# Missing Asset Generation Prompts

Audit date: 2026-05-25 Asia/Shanghai.

Scope: planning only. This document adds prompts, expected ids, and player-facing description mapping for the missing asset classes identified in `docs/assets/asset-inventory-and-gap-list.md`. It does not generate images, add binary assets, register manifest entries, or change runtime selection code.

Primary source order:

- Gap baseline: `docs/assets/asset-inventory-and-gap-list.md`.
- Pipeline and inventory rules: `docs/ASSET_PIPELINE.md`, `docs/ASSET_INVENTORY.md`, `docs/assets/asset-expansion-roadmap-2026-05-24.md`.
- Runtime binding style: `assets/manifest.json`, `assets/generated/manifest.json`, `src/core/itemCatalog.js`, `src/core/rules.js`, `src/core/assetSelection.js`, `src/core/localization.js`, and `src/core/statusEffects.js`.

## Planning Counts

| Planned set | Count | Status |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Single-scene prompts | 66 | `ready-for-generation`, not generated |
| Sprite/cutout sheets | 18 | `ready-for-generation`, not generated |
| Planned icon/cutout cells | 768 | `ready-for-generation`, not generated |
| Description map rows | 834 | `ready-for-generation`, not generated or integrated |

Sheet ids in this file intentionally start at `042` to avoid colliding with the existing roadmap's proposed scene wave around `034-041`. If a coordinator reserves different ids before generation, keep the prompt refs stable and renumber only the sheet id, file name, and asset id prefix together.

Extension wave `050-059` was added on 2026-05-25 as planning-only queue expansion. It intentionally avoids the actively assigned `042-049` generation and slicing range. New single-scene refs start at `scene-050-01`; new icon sheet refs start at `icon-sheet-050-*` and run through `icon-sheet-059-*`.

## Existing Description Sources

| Asset family | Existing source to reuse | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Runtime item and spell-scroll descriptions | `src/core/itemCatalog.js` `ITEM_CATALOG` | Existing scroll/item copy is player-facing and bilingual. Planned rows only add descriptions for new assets or visual variants. |
| Runtime spell names and rules metadata | `src/core/rules.js` `SPELLS`, `src/core/localization.js` `spellLabels` | Spell definitions have school, tier, category, action, tags, and localized names. They do not currently carry long visual descriptions. |
| Runtime status labels | `src/core/statusEffects.js` `STATUS_EFFECTS` | Runtime status labels exist for 12 conditions. Missing/exact aliases stay planned until condition definitions or aliases are added. |
| Generated spell/status descriptions | `assets/generated/manifest.json` | Existing player-safe generated spell and status icons already include bilingual manifest descriptions. |
| Scene metadata and descriptions | `assets/generated/manifest.json` | Existing scene rows include stage descriptions, weather, time, mood, threat, taxonomy, and soundscape hints. New scenes must follow that shape. |

## Icon Style Anchor

Use this anchor at the start of every icon, badge, status, cutout, and overlay sheet prompt:

> AIDM unified tabletop icon style: painterly fantasy RPG cutout icons, three-quarter front view, crisp readable silhouette, centered object occupying 72-82 percent of each tile, consistent thin dark bronze rim light, soft contact shadow inside the object only, high-detail materials with leather, brass, vellum, glass, enamel, stone, smoke, and magical glow, saturated but controlled palette with warm brass, deep teal, muted crimson, ivory parchment, silver blue, moss green, and charcoal shadows, no text, no numbers, no UI labels, no watermark, no frame, no transparent background, flat pure chroma-key green `#00ff00` background in every tile, 12 percent safe padding around each icon, consistent light from upper left, game-ready readable at 64px.

Negative prompt to reuse for icon sheets:

> text, letters, numbers, watermark, logo, signature, UI frame, border box, transparent background, alpha checkerboard, gradient background, scenery background, cropped object, object touching tile edges, duplicate cells, mixed art styles, photoreal product photo, 3D render, blurry icon, low contrast, tiny unreadable details, humans or faces unless the cell explicitly asks for a badge silhouette.

## Scene Prompt Rules

Generate scenes as individual full-bleed images, not sprite sheets. If the ingester needs a sheet source, treat each generated image as a `1x1` preserved tile.

- Suggested source size: `1536x1024`, 3:2 full-bleed.
- Runtime category: `scenes`.
- Expected group after review: `generated-scenes`.
- Expected surfaces after review: `stage-backdrop`, `relevant-scene`.
- Target source path pattern: `assets/generated/sheets/<assetId>.png`.
- Target frame path pattern after ingest: `assets/generated/scenes/<assetId>.png`.
- Scene semantic key pattern: `scene.backbone.<location>.<weather>.<time>.<state>.v01`.
- Scene negative prompt: text, labels, UI, frame, logo, watermark, isolated item icon, character portrait, modern signage, unreadable map text, split contact sheet, sprite sheet, black bars, extreme blur, over-dark foreground, cropped focal point.

## Spell Completeness Matrix

`src/core/rules.js` currently defines 32 spells. Existing generated spell art has 16 unique icons from `aidm-spells-sheet-015`; `src/core/assetSelection.js` maps or aliases 19 spell ids to those 16 concepts. This plan treats the strict uniqueness gap as 16 visual deficits: 13 spells with no stable unique generated concept plus 3 high-priority alias splits. `sleep` remains a documented alias split candidate because `Ashen Shroud` may stay acceptable if the status sheet adds stronger `drowsy` and scroll visuals.

## Later Integration Steps

1. Image generation: generate the 16 single scenes and 8 sprite sheets from this document; save source files under `assets/generated/sheets/`.
2. Cutting and alpha: ingest single scenes as `1x1 --preserve-tile`; ingest icon/cutout sheets with `--chroma-key`; verify RGBA alpha for every cutout.
3. Manifest registration: add real `generatedSheets` and `rasterAssets` only after files exist; default new frames to `internal` and `catalog-internal`.
4. Description map binding: convert the rows above into metadata plan entries with bilingual `displayName`, `description`, `semanticKey`, `variantAxes`, and exact `gameplayBinding`.
5. Runtime ownership: expose player-safe assets only after a scene, item, spell, status, class, profession, action, or soundscape owner exists.
6. UI/game surface integration: bind to exact surfaces, for example `stage-backdrop`, `relevant-scene`, `spell-card`, `status-icon`, `inventory-item`, `market-item`, `reward-card`, `item-detail`, `character-builder`, or `player-detail`.
7. Verification: run manifest/file checks, duplicate semantic-key checks, alpha/full-bleed checks, generated asset tests, and focused UI/browser smoke for the surfaces that consume the new bindings.

## Non-Goals

- Do not copy DND/SRD rules text into prompts or runtime descriptions. Use school, tier, condition, and class terms only as taxonomy labels.
- Do not expose these planned assets as player-safe before generation, slicing, manifest metadata, runtime binding, and review.
- Do not add generated file placeholders, manifest placeholder raster rows, or broad player-facing galleries.
